**What and why**
Both **Unified and Side-by-side** views are in v0.1 (d0017). Add the segmented toggle and the side-by-side renderer in
`crewboard-app`, and the edge states from the frames: a **large diff** (54 files) that stays fast, **binary** files (no text
diff), **renamed** files (old → new path), and an **empty diff** state.

**Done when**
- [x] a Unified / Side-by-side toggle switches the view and is remembered globally
- [x] side-by-side aligns old and new lines with highlighting, line numbers on both sides
- [x] a 54-file diff scrolls smoothly; files render on demand
- [x] binary files show "binary file · size"; renames show old → new path; an empty diff shows the empty state from the frame
- [ ] tests: view-model tests for line alignment and the edge-state selection; checked by hand against the named frames
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REV-02 and decision 0017

### T-0093 · Line comments UI
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** REV-03
- **Decisions:** 0017
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 07 · Diff, review, approve & send back → Diff tab · SHOP-18 · unified (panel expanded), Side-by-side
- **Depends on:** T-0085, T-0091
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
In both diff views the user adds a comment on a line, edits or deletes it, and replies in a thread on the same line (d0017). A
**pending counter** ("3 comments pending") sits next to Send back. Comments are stored by T-0085. **No partial approval**: there
is no per-file or per-comment approve.

**Done when**
- [x] clicking a line gutter opens a comment box anchored to file, line and side; Save / Cancel; ⌘↵ saves
- [x] existing comments show inline as threads; pending ones can be edited and deleted, sent ones are read-only with their round
- [x] the pending counter updates live and is visible on the Diff tab and next to Send back
- [x] works in unified and side-by-side views
- [ ] tests: view-model tests for add/edit/delete/thread and the counter; checked by hand against the named frames
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REV-03 and decision 0017

### T-0094 · Send back dialog
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** REV-04
- **Decisions:** 0034, 0048, 0058
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 07 · Diff, review, approve & send back → Send back · review & confirm
- **Depends on:** T-0086, T-0093
- **Size:** S

**What and why**
**Send back** (⌘⌫, in the Diff tab and the panel action bar) opens a dialog listing the bundled comments and an optional general
note, then calls T-0086. The ticket moves straight to Dev and the card shows "Sent back · N comments · round K" (d0034). If the
session's input line has unsent text, the unsent-text prompt from T-0061 appears first (d0048). Not shown on epics.

**Done when**
- [x] ⌘⌫ (and the button) open the dialog with every pending comment (file:line + text) and a note field; Send / Cancel
- [x] Send calls T-0086; on "needs confirmation" the unsent-text prompt (Keep it and cancel / Clear it and send) is shown
- [x] after sending, the panel switches to the Terminal tab and the card shows the sent-back marker
- [x] sending with zero comments and an empty note is allowed (the note is optional, REV-04)
- [x] hidden on epics and on tickets not in Ready for Review
- [ ] tests: view-model tests for the dialog states; checked by hand against the named frame
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REV-04 and decisions 0034, 0048, 0058

### T-0095 · Approve ▾ and Local merge UI
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** GIT-03, REV-08, UI-02
- **Decisions:** 0015, 0038, 0050, 0058, 0053
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 07 · Diff, review, approve & send back → Local merge · confirm, Local merge · success, Merge conflict during Local merge, Local merge · rebasing onto main… (0038), Local merge · refused, uncommitted changes (0038), Uncommitted files · refused; 03 · Board & columns → Approve choice on drop
- **Depends on:** T-0060, T-0089, T-0090
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
**Approve ▾** (⌘↵, panel and Diff tab) offers **Local merge** and **Open PR** (d0015); Open PR is shown disabled with "comes in
M10" until T-0149, and only on delivery units. Dragging a card from Ready for Review into Approved opens the same choice as a
popover. Local merge shows a confirmation with the **target branch** and an **editable squash message**, then "Rebasing onto
main…", then success (toast "Merged into main"; "2 waiting tickets started" when the scheduler starts dependents) or the
conflict state with **Send to agent to fix**. Refusals: "shop-web has uncommitted changes on main. Clean up and retry." and
"SHOP-19 has 2 uncommitted files" with **View** (opens the file list in the Diff tab) and **Send back** (REV-08).

**Done when**
- [x] ⌘↵ and the Approve ▾ button open the menu; dropping into Approved opens the approve-choice popover; cancelling the popover leaves the ticket in Ready for Review
- [x] Local merge confirm shows target (`main`, or the epic branch for an epic child) and the editable message; Confirm calls T-0089
- [x] each result has its state from the frames: rebasing, success toast, conflict (with Send to agent to fix → T-0090), dirty checkout, diverged base, uncommitted files (View / Send back)
- [x] a toast "N waiting tickets started" appears when the scheduler starts dependents after the merge
- [x] nothing pushes; no state offers "stash" or "discard"
- [ ] tests: view-model tests for every result state; checked by hand against the named frames
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names GIT-03, REV-08, UI-02 and decisions 0015, 0038, 0050, 0053

**Notes:** the "changed since review" warning before approve is T-0098; Open PR is enabled in T-0149.

### T-0096 · Early conflict flag
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** GIT-04
- **Decisions:** 0038, 0051
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0089
- **Size:** S

**What and why**
After every merge into a branch, Crewboard runs a **read-only test merge** for every ticket in Ready for Review that targets that
branch (d0038). Tickets that would conflict get **"conflicts with `<target>`"** (for example "conflicts with `develop`", d0051),
so the user sees it before clicking Approve. No files are touched and no tokens are spent. Implement it in `crewboard-core`'s git
module with a method that doesn't modify any worktree or ref (e.g. `git merge-tree`).

**Done when**
- [x] after T-0089 merges into a branch, every Ready for Review ticket targeting that branch is test-merged and its flag set or cleared
- [x] the test merge changes no worktree, index, branch or ref (verified in tests)
- [x] the flag is cleared when the ticket's branch changes and the next test merge is clean
- [x] tests: temp git repos — SHOP-18 and SHOP-19 both editing `lib/pricing.ts`: merging one flags the other; a non-conflicting ticket stays unflagged; refs unchanged after the check
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names GIT-04 and decisions 0038, 0051

**Notes:** name the git command and the oldest git version it needs in the code and the PR (for example `git merge-tree
--write-tree` needs git 2.38 or newer; the author's machine has 2.54). No decision sets a minimum git version, and the tools
checklist (T-0013) doesn't check one.

### T-0097 · Changed since review (core)
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** REV-06, GIT-05
- **Decisions:** 0043
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0089
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
A ticket in Review can still change: an agent question or the user's typing can wake its session (d0043). When a ticket enters
Ready for Review, record its branch head (`review_head_sha`, `review_entered_at`). Before any approve (Local merge, Open PR, Push
to PR), check for **new commits**: if any, the approve returns **"SHOP-18 changed since you reviewed it · 1 new commit"** with a
diff of **only the new commits**, and the user chooses **Approve anyway** or **Send back**. **Crewboard's own rebases don't count**
(GIT-05): compare content, not commit IDs, for Crewboard-made rewrites.

**Done when**
- [x] entering Ready for Review stores the head; a new review round stores it again
- [x] `approve.check(key)` returns `Unchanged` or `Changed{new_commits, diff}`; the approve requests require an explicit `approve_anyway` after `Changed`
- [x] a Crewboard rebase (T-0089 step 3, T-0101) records the rebased head as reviewed when the resulting tree equals the reviewed content rebased (no warning)
- [x] the diff of only new commits is produced from the recorded head to the current head
- [x] tests: temp git repos — no new commits; one new commit (warning, diff contains only it); Crewboard rebase onto a moved base (no warning)
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REV-06, GIT-05 and decision 0043

**Notes:** the push-time content comparison is T-0145 (M10).

### T-0098 · Conflict flag and changed-since-review UI
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** GIT-04, REV-06
- **Decisions:** 0038, 0043
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 07 · Diff, review, approve & send back → Approve · changed since review (0043); 05 · Ticket side panel → SHOP-18 · conflicts with main; 04 · Ticket card variants · Light / 04 · Ticket card variants · Dark → Review · conflicts with main
- **Depends on:** T-0095, T-0096, T-0097
- **Size:** S

**What and why**
Show the **"conflicts with `main`"** flag on Review cards (pill per the flag priority of T-0031) and in the panel header, with
Send to agent to fix in the panel. Before every approve, call T-0097: if the ticket changed, show **"SHOP-18 changed since you
reviewed it · 1 new commit"**, the diff of only the new commits, and **Approve anyway** / **Send back**.

**Done when**
- [x] cards and panel show the conflict flag from T-0096 and update live from events
- [x] Approve (Local merge here; Open PR / Push to PR reuse it in M10) opens the changed-since-review dialog when needed; Approve anyway continues, Send back opens T-0094
- [ ] tests: view-model tests for both flags; checked by hand against the named frames
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names GIT-04, REV-06 and decisions 0038, 0043

### T-0099 · Done cleanup: worktree, ports, Run/Shell hooks
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** REC-05
- **Decisions:** 0018, 0035, 0026
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0089
- **Size:** S

**What and why**
Reaching **Done** is the user's click (Local merge, later Mark merged), so pure bookkeeping follows automatically (d0018): the
ticket's **worktree is removed** (the branch is kept) **unless it has uncommitted changes**, in which case it is kept and flagged
for the Cleanup view (T-0116); its **ports are freed** (d0026); its agent session ends; Run stops (toast) and the Shell closes
(d0035). Run and Shell are built in M9 (T-0133, T-0137): here, add the "ticket finished" hook they register with.

**Done when**
- [x] on Done · Merged: the agent session ends, the worktree is removed with `git worktree remove` when clean, the branch is kept, both ports return to the pool
- [x] a dirty worktree is never removed; the ticket gets a "worktree kept: uncommitted changes" flag that Cleanup lists
- [x] a `on_ticket_finished` hook exists for Run/Shell to stop/close; until M9 it has no subscribers
- [x] the same routine is reusable by Close ticket (T-0100)
- [x] tests: temp git repos — clean worktree removed, branch kept; dirty worktree kept and flagged; ports freed
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REC-05 and decisions 0018, 0035, 0026

### T-0100 · Close ticket (any state), bulk close for epics
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** CLS-01, CLS-02, EPC-06
- **Decisions:** 0033, 0044
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0099
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
**Close ticket** works from any state (d0033): if an agent runs, the request needs a confirmation flag ("it will be stopped
first"), then the agent is stopped. On close: the **branch is kept**; the worktree is removed **unless it has uncommitted changes**
(then flagged for Cleanup); Run/Shell stop (T-0099 routine); the ticket becomes **Done · Closed · not merged**. **Mark closed** on a
PR ticket (wired in M10, T-0147) uses the same path (CLS-02). Closing an **epic** with unfinished children runs as one request
that lists the children to close and the agents to stop, then closes them all; merged children stay merged (d0044, EPC-06).

**Done when**
- [x] `ticket.close(key, confirm)` closes from every state in §7.2; with a running agent and no `confirm` it returns "needs confirmation · agent will be stopped"
- [x] branch kept, worktree removed unless dirty (flagged), ports freed, `outcome = closed`, History entry
- [x] `epic.close_plan(key)` (from T-0025) is executed by `epic.close(key, confirm)`: unfinished children closed, running agents stopped, merged children untouched; an epic with nothing merged ends "Closed · not merged"
- [x] closing never deletes a branch or a dirty worktree
- [x] tests: temp git repos — close from Backlog, Dev with a running session (ordinary command), Ready for Review; dirty worktree kept; epic with one merged, one working, one Backlog child
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names CLS-01, CLS-02, EPC-06 and decisions 0033, 0044

**Notes:** questions Q4, resolved: decision 0033 wins (worktree removed unless it has uncommitted changes); the *Close epic ·
confirm* text is fixed by design update H.

### T-0101 · Reopen Closed tickets, free rebase on the way to Dev
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** CLS-03, GIT-06
- **Decisions:** 0044, 0033, 0005
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0088, T-0100
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
**Only Closed tickets can be reopened** (d0044): dragging out of Done or "Reopen" moves the ticket to Backlog; **Merged tickets are
locked** (fixing merged work means a new ticket). Reopening any epic child returns its epic to Backlog. When a reopened ticket is
dragged to Dev, its **old branch is reused** and first **rebased onto the current base** with no tokens (GIT-06), using the
checkpoint rule (T-0088); the worktree is recreated if it was removed. The agent then starts in a **new `work-ticket` session**,
**not** a resume of the stored conversation (d0044, questions Q5). If the rebase conflicted, `crewboard prime` tells the agent to
resolve that first with the `restack` skill. Implement this in `crewboard-core`.

**Done when**
- [x] `ticket.reopen(key)` moves Done · Closed → Backlog; Done · Merged is refused ("Merged tickets are locked. Create a new ticket.")
- [x] reopening an epic child moves its epic back to Backlog
- [x] dragging a reopened ticket to Dev reuses its branch, recreates the worktree at its key path if missing, runs `rebase_with_checkpoint` onto the current base and records the new `base_sha`, then goes through the normal start sequence (T-0053) with a **new** agent session; the stored Claude session IDs stay in History, and `claude --resume` is never used for this path
- [x] a rebase conflict leaves the branch as it was before the rebase attempt, records a "conflict with `<base>`" fact on the ticket, and the agent still starts
- [x] `crewboard prime` for that ticket opens with "Reopened: the rebase onto `main` conflicted. Resolve it first with the restack skill." (with the conflicting files), and says nothing when the rebase was clean; the fact clears once the agent submits again
- [x] tests: temp git repos — reopen then start with a clean rebase (new session recorded, previous session ID kept); with a conflict (prime text present); Merged refused; epic returns to Backlog
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names CLS-03, GIT-06 and decisions 0044, 0033, 0005

**Notes:** questions Q5, resolved → option 2 (new session; conflict announced through `crewboard prime`). The `restack` skill comes
from T-0090. Session launch reuses T-0052/T-0053 unchanged.

### T-0102 · Closed parent
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** CLS-04
- **Decisions:** 0033, 0044
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0077, T-0100
- **Size:** S

**What and why**
A Closed parent **never counts as ready** (d0044). Queued children of a closed parent show **"parent SHOP-26 closed"** and get
two user actions: **Reopen parent** and **Remove dependency**. Crewboard doesn't decide (d0033). Add the state to readiness
(T-0077) and the two requests in `crewboard-core`; the inbox item comes with the inbox (M7, group 2).

**Done when**
- [x] a Queued ticket with any Closed parent carries the "parent `<KEY>` closed" state (names every closed parent)
- [x] "Reopen parent" calls T-0101's reopen on the parent; "Remove dependency" removes the link and re-evaluates readiness
- [x] the state is listed by the needs-you derivation (T-0078) as group 2 "Parent closed"
- [x] tests: unit tests for readiness with a closed parent, reopen, and removal
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names CLS-04 and decisions 0033, 0044

### T-0103 · Submit for review by the user (core)
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** CLS-05
- **Decisions:** 0052, 0050
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0072
- **Size:** S

**What and why**
The user can finish a ticket themselves (d0052): **Submit for review** is available when the agent isn't mid-turn (idle, orange
or Interrupted). It runs the **same checks** as the agent's `review submit` (refuses uncommitted files, d0050), stores the summary
**"Submitted by you"** plus an optional note, and moves the ticket to Ready for Review. The session stays alive so Send back works
as usual.

**Done when**
- [x] `ticket.submit_by_user(key, note)` succeeds from Idle, Asks you, Needs you and Interrupted; refused while the agent is working (mid-turn) or in background
- [x] uncommitted files refuse with "2 files are not committed. Commit them, or discard them if they're scratch files"
- [x] the review summary reads "Submitted by you" plus the note; the session is not stopped; the review head is recorded (T-0097)
- [x] tests: unit tests for allowed/refused states; temp git repo for the uncommitted refusal
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names CLS-05 and decisions 0052, 0050

### T-0104 · Park and resume the same conversation (core)
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** CLS-06
- **Decisions:** 0052, 0026
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0053
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
**Park** = drag Dev → Backlog after a confirmation ("SHOP-25's agent will be stopped. Its branch, files and conversation are
kept.", d0052). Crewboard stops the agent, keeps the branch, the worktree (including uncommitted files) and the Claude session ID,
**frees both ports**, and the card shows **"Parked · has work"**; it doesn't count toward the optional limit. Dragging it back to
Dev **resumes the same conversation** (`claude --resume`) with **new ports** ("Resuming conversation…"). The drag is the go-ahead.

**Done when**
- [x] `ticket.park(key, confirm)` from Dev: stops the session, keeps branch/worktree/session id, frees ports, sets `parked`; without `confirm` returns "needs confirmation"
- [x] a parked ticket entering Queued resumes via `claude --resume <stored id>` in the same worktree with newly allocated ports instead of the fresh start
- [x] parked tickets are excluded from `max_concurrency` counts
- [x] tests: PTY test with an ordinary command standing in for the session (stopped, resumed with the stored id in the argument list); ports freed and re-allocated; worktree files kept
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names CLS-06 and decisions 0052, 0026

**Notes:** a resume that can't find the conversation follows Resume failed (T-0112).

### T-0105 · Epic lifecycle: to Review, Closed · not merged, Local merge of the epic
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** EPC-04, EPC-05
- **Decisions:** 0059, 0044
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0089, T-0100
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
Epics are delivery units (d0059). When **every child is Done** (merged into the epic branch, or closed, with at least one merged),
the epic moves to **Ready for Review** automatically (bookkeeping). If every child ended Closed, it becomes **"Closed · not
merged"** (d0044). Epic review uses the whole-feature diff (T-0084). Approve on an epic: **Local merge** into its base branch with
the same rules as T-0089 (Open PR comes in M10). **No Send back** on an epic: "Add or reopen a child ticket to change it". Adding
or reopening a child returns the epic to Backlog until that child is merged.

**Done when**
- [x] child state changes re-evaluate the epic: all Done with ≥1 merged → Ready for Review; all Closed → Done · Closed · not merged
- [x] epic Local merge squash-merges the epic branch into its base through T-0089 (fetch/ff/diverged, dirty checkout refusal, never pushes)
- [x] Send back on an epic is refused with the message above
- [x] adding a child (or reopening one) to an epic in Review or Approved returns it to Backlog
- [x] tests: temp git repos — epic with 3 children reaching Review; all closed; epic Local merge; new child returns epic to Backlog
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names EPC-04, EPC-05 and decisions 0059, 0044

### T-0106 · Close / Reopen / Submit for review UI
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** CLS-01, CLS-03, CLS-05
- **Decisions:** 0033, 0044, 0052
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 05 · Ticket side panel → Close ticket · panel "…" menu, Close ticket · confirm, Submit for review · dialog, Closed · not merged · Reopen, Dark · closed ticket; 04 · Ticket card variants · Light / 04 · Ticket card variants · Dark → Done · closed, not merged, Review · submitted by you
- **Depends on:** T-0060, T-0100, T-0101, T-0103
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
Add the panel's **"…" menu** entries **Close ticket** (confirmation that names the running agent, d0033) and **Submit for review**
(dialog with optional note, available only when the agent isn't mid-turn, d0052). Done · Closed cards fade with a **Reopen**
affordance; dragging a Closed card out of Done to Backlog reopens it; Merged cards refuse the drag with "not allowed" feedback
(d0044). The Review card shows "Submitted by you".

**Done when**
- [x] "…" menu shows Close ticket in every state and Submit for review only in allowed states (disabled with a tooltip otherwise)
- [x] the close confirmation says the agent will be stopped first when one runs; the submit dialog shows the uncommitted-files refusal inline
- [x] Closed · not merged cards show Reopen; drag Done (Closed) → Backlog reopens; Merged → Backlog shows forbidden feedback
- [ ] tests: view-model tests for menu availability per state; checked by hand against the named frames
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names CLS-01, CLS-03, CLS-05 and decisions 0033, 0044, 0052

### T-0109 · Epic review UI
- **Epic:** M6 · Review & Local merge
- **Requirements:** EPC-03, EPC-04, EPC-05
- **Decisions:** 0059
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 05 · Ticket side panel → Epic panel · SHOP-40 Wishlist, Epic in Review · Diff by child (SHOP-40), Approve ▾ on an epic; 07 · Diff, review, approve & send back → Epic child · Approve offers only Local merge; 04 · Ticket card variants · Light / 04 · Ticket card variants · Dark → Row · epics as delivery units

The epic card moves Backlog ("1 / 3 merged") → Ready for Review → Approved → Done like a ticket (d0059). Its panel's Diff tab
shows the whole feature (SHOP-40 vs `main`) **grouped by child**. **Approve ▾** on an epic offers Local merge (Open PR from M10),
with no Send back and the note "Add or reopen a child ticket to change it". An **epic child's** Approve offers **only Local merge**,
into the epic branch.

**Done when**
- [x] epic panel shows External ID (PAY-123), progress, child list and the diff grouped by child
- [x] Approve ▾ on an epic: Local merge enabled, Open PR shown for M10, Send back absent with the explanatory note

## M7 · Attention & recovery
Goal: one global inbox answers "what needs me?", the focused terminal stays quiet, and after a reboot or service restart every
ticket is reconciled, shown as Interrupted where needed, resumable by click, and leftovers can be cleaned up by hand.

### T-0110 · Focused-terminal rule (core)
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** ATT-03
- **Decisions:** 0042
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0055, T-0058, T-0067
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
While a ticket's terminal has focus — in the app or through `crewboard attach` — that ticket raises **no orange, no inbox item and
no OS notification** (d0042). If the user leaves the terminal while the agent still waits, it turns orange **at that moment**. The
service in `crewboard-core` tracks focus per session from every client (app focus/blur messages, attach connect/detach) and applies
it when deriving the orange family and needs-you items.

**Done when**
- [x] clients report focus gained/lost per session; `crewboard attach` counts as focused while attached; a client disconnect counts as focus lost
- [x] while focused, Asks you / Needs you / Idle are recorded but suppressed (no orange, no inbox item, no notification); a "your turn" signal is exposed instead
- [x] on focus lost with the agent still waiting, the orange state, inbox item and notification eligibility appear immediately
- [x] tests: unit tests feeding real-shaped hook JSON while focused, then losing focus; two clients (app + attach) where one leaves
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names ATT-03 and decision 0042

### T-0111 · Startup reconcile and Interrupted
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** REC-01, SVC-03
- **Decisions:** 0018, 0045, 0022
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0053, T-0067
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
When the service starts (after a reboot, a crash or a user-clicked restart, d0022), it compares every live ticket with its session
and worktree (d0018). Alive → reattach silently. Gone → by state (d0045): **Dev · working / background / limit-wait →
Interrupted** with an inbox item; **Dev · orange → Interrupted, keeping the label and question**; **Ready for Review and Approved →
not Interrupted**, no inbox item, not in Resume all, History notes "session ended (restart)"; Run and Shell → "stopped by restart".
**Nothing resumes automatically.** Implement the reconcile table in `crewboard-core` as a pure function plus the startup step.

**Done when**
- [x] a pure `reconcile(state_before, session_alive) -> outcome` implements the REC-01 table and is unit-tested row by row
- [x] startup applies it to every ticket; the service records whether this start followed an unclean stop (for REC-06's summary)
- [x] orange tickets keep their label and question text alongside Interrupted
- [x] Run/Shell sessions are marked "stopped by restart" through the hook point used by M9; nothing is relaunched
- [x] a user-clicked restart (T-0012) leads to the same reconcile
- [x] tests: unit tests for the table; integration test that kills the daemon with a live ordinary-command session and restarts it
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REC-01, SVC-03 and decisions 0018, 0045, 0022

**Notes:** questions Q2, resolved: questions from other agents to Review/Approved tickets after a restart follow §16 / MSG-03
(M10); decision 0045's wording was fixed.

### T-0112 · Resume, Resume all, Resume failed, Start from scratch
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** REC-01, REC-03
- **Decisions:** 0018, 0045
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0111
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
**Resume** = `claude --resume <stored id>` in a new session in the same worktree (d0018). **Resume all** covers only the Dev
tickets that were Interrupted (not Start interrupted, not Review/Approved, d0045). If the CLI can't find the conversation,
the ticket shows **Resume failed** with **Retry** and **Start from scratch** (a new `claude` session in the same worktree after
confirmation; keeps branch, commits and files; stores the new session ID; History "Started from scratch (resume failed)" with the
old id). **Never falls back on its own.** Implement in `crewboard-core`.

**Done when**
- [x] `ticket.resume(key)` and `tickets.resume_all()` start resumed sessions only on request; Resume all lists exactly the eligible tickets
- [x] a resume whose process exits immediately with the "no conversation found" message is classified as Resume failed; other exits keep their own states
- [x] `ticket.retry_resume(key)` re-runs the same resume; `ticket.start_from_scratch(key, confirm)` launches a fresh agent session in the same worktree and stores the new id
- [x] every session id stays in `sessions` (none overwritten)
- [x] tests: PTY tests with an ordinary command standing in for `claude` (one that exits with the not-found message, one that keeps running); Resume all eligibility unit tests
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REC-01, REC-03 and decisions 0018, 0045

**Notes:** the exact "no conversation found" wording must be verified against the installed CLI by hand (see *16 · Recovery &
cleanup → Note for the implementing agent*).

### T-0113 · "Before the restart" reader
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** REC-02
- **Decisions:** 0045, 0020
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0052
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
For an Interrupted ticket, the Terminal tab shows the agent's last messages and actions, read **read-only from Claude Code's
saved conversation** for the stored session ID (d0045) — `~/.claude/projects/<folder for the worktree path>/<session-id>.jsonl`.
Crewboard **never copies terminal output to disk**. If the conversation is gone, return "conversation no longer available". Like
usage (d0020), a format change may break this display, never a workflow.

**Done when**
- [x] `session.before_restart(ticket)` returns the last N user/assistant messages and tool actions (name + short summary) from the transcript
- [x] missing or unreadable transcript → `NotAvailable`; unknown record shapes are skipped, never an error that blocks the ticket
- [x] the reader never writes to Claude Code's folders
- [x] tests: fixture transcript files (real-shaped JSONL); missing file; malformed lines
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REC-02 and decisions 0045, 0020

**Notes:** the transcript location and record format must be verified against the installed Claude Code by hand (SDD §26, P8).

### T-0114 · Inbox answers: type into the session; resume and deliver for Interrupted
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** ATT-08
- **Decisions:** 0045, 0048
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0054, T-0078, T-0112
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
Answering an Asks you / Idle item from the inbox types the answer into the session (d0048): it is the user's click, so it is never
held, but unsent text triggers *Keep it and cancel / Clear it and send*. For an **Interrupted orange** ticket the same click
**resumes the session and delivers the answer** in one step (d0045); permission prompts simply ask again after Resume. Implement
the `inbox.answer` request in `crewboard-core`.

**Done when**
- [x] `inbox.answer(key, text, clear)` types the text + Enter through the user-click delivery path; with unsent text and no `clear` it returns "needs confirmation"
- [x] for an Interrupted orange ticket it resumes (T-0112) and delivers once the resumed CLI is ready for input
- [x] the answer clears the orange state (ATT-02) and the inbox item
- [x] tests: PTY tests with an ordinary command (cat-like) checking the typed text; resume-then-deliver ordering
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names ATT-08 and decisions 0045, 0048

### T-0115 · Inbox events, counts and resolution
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** ATT-06, ATT-09, ATT-10
- **Decisions:** 0016, 0024
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0078, T-0110
- **Size:** S

**What and why**
Expose the global needs-you queue (derived in T-0078) over the protocol: `inbox.list` across all projects in the three groups,
an "inbox changed" event, and counts **globally and per project** for the header and the project switcher (ATT-10, d0024). Items
that resolve elsewhere (e.g. answered through `crewboard attach`) disappear on the next derivation (ATT-09). Add a suppression
hook for limit-stopped agents so no items appear while the plan-limit banner is active (wired in M8, T-0129).

**Done when**
- [x] `inbox.list` returns grouped, ordered items with project, key, title, reason, age, blocked count; `inbox.counts` returns total + per project
- [x] an "inbox changed" event is published whenever the derived set changes (and not otherwise)
- [x] an item answered outside the inbox disappears within one event; focused tickets are excluded (T-0110)
- [x] a `suppress_limit_stopped` switch removes items of agents marked "Waiting for limit reset"
- [x] tests: unit tests for counts per project, resolution, suppression
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names ATT-06, ATT-09, ATT-10 and decisions 0016, 0024

### T-0116 · Cleanup scan and delete (core)
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** REC-04
- **Decisions:** 0018, 0026
- **Design:** —
- **Depends on:** T-0046, T-0099
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
The Cleanup view lists leftovers that no ticket owns (d0018): **orphaned sessions**, **worktrees** under `~/.crewboard/wt`,
**branches** `crewboard/*` with no ticket, **leaked ports** (d0026), and **Done/Closed tickets whose worktree was kept** because of
uncommitted changes. Each item has details (size, path, age). **Nothing is deleted automatically**: deletion runs only on an
explicit request listing the selected items. Implement scan and delete in `crewboard-core`.

**Done when**
- [x] `cleanup.scan()` returns grouped items with size, path, age and why it's listed; it changes nothing
- [x] `cleanup.delete(items, confirm)` deletes only the listed items; a worktree with uncommitted changes is deleted only when it is explicitly listed
- [x] branches are deleted with `git branch -D` only for listed `crewboard/*` branches; the user's own branches are never candidates
- [x] nothing in the service calls delete on its own (reviewed in the PR)
- [x] tests: temp git repos — orphan worktree, orphan branch, kept dirty worktree of a Closed ticket, leaked port; scan is side-effect free; delete removes only the selection
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names REC-04 and decisions 0018, 0026

### T-0117 · OS notifications (macOS) and setting
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** ATT-11
- **Decisions:** 0016, 0042
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 11 · Banners, toasts & OS notifications → OS notifications; 15 · Settings → Global · Usage, notifications, data, appearance (Notifications)
- **Depends on:** T-0045, T-0115
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
OS notifications go out **only for items that block an agent** — orange items and Interrupted (d0016) — and **never for the ticket
whose terminal has focus** (d0042). Clicking a notification opens that ticket with its terminal focused. A global setting turns them
off (on by default). One notification per item, not per event.

**Done when**
- [x] new group-1 items and Interrupted tickets post one macOS notification each (permission prompt, agent question, session interrupted texts from the frame)
- [x] no notification for Ready for Review, focused tickets or limit-stopped agents; no repeats for the same item
- [ ] clicking opens the app, the ticket's panel and focuses its terminal
- [x] Settings → Notifications toggle (on by default) stored per T-0028 and respected immediately
- [ ] tests: unit tests for the "should notify" decision; checked by hand on macOS against the frame
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names ATT-11 and decisions 0016, 0042

**Notes:** Windows and Linux notifications are T-0159 (M11).

### T-0118 · Inbox surface
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** UI-06, ATT-06, ATT-09, ATT-10
- **Decisions:** 0016, 0024, 0058
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 10 · Needs-you inbox → Inbox popover · mixed, 2 projects, In context · 1440 window, One item, Empty · nothing needs you, Resolved elsewhere, Inbox count in header & switcher, Dark · inbox, Resume all · confirm, Parent closed · new item in group 2
- **Depends on:** T-0033, T-0115
- **Size:** L

**What and why**
The inbox is reachable from anywhere with **⇧⌘I** and the header button: groups **Asks you · Needs you · Idle**, **Interrupted &
parent closed** (with **Resume all** and its confirmation listing the tickets), and **Ready for Review**, each with counts; items
show project, key, title, reason, age and "blocks N" (d0016). The header button and the project rail badges show the same counts
(ATT-10). Resolved items fade out gracefully; the empty state reads "Nothing needs you". Parent closed items offer Reopen parent /
Remove dependency.

**Done when**
- [x] ⇧⌘I and the header button open the popover; Esc closes; items open the ticket panel

- [x] Resume all confirms with the list and resumes only interrupted sessions; Start interrupted items show their own Retry start
- [x] header count = sum of rail badges; resolved-elsewhere items fade out without a jump
- [ ] tests: view-model tests for grouping, counts and fading; checked by hand against the named frames
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names UI-06, ATT-06, ATT-09, ATT-10 and decisions 0016, 0024, 0058

### T-0119 · Next-item hotkey (⌘J)
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
- **Requirements:** ATT-07
- **Decisions:** 0016, 0058
- **Design:** crewboard.pen → 10 · Needs-you inbox → 1 · ⌘J from anywhere, 2 · Answered, 3 · Next item, 4 · All clear
- **Depends on:** T-0058, T-0118
- **Size:** M

**What and why**
**⌘J** opens the next inbox item's panel with its **terminal focused**, from anywhere, even with the inbox closed (d0016). It
**doesn't auto-advance**: after answering, the ticket stays open so the user can watch the agent react, and they press ⌘J again.
Show "3 of 7" progress and an "all clear" state. ⌘J works while a terminal has focus (macOS ⌘ never reaches the terminal, d0058).

- [x] ⌘J opens the first item in inbox order with the terminal focused; the next ⌘J moves to the next item
- [x] answering never moves focus or opens another ticket by itself
- [x] "N of M" progress is shown while stepping; the all-clear state appears when nothing is left
- [ ] tests: view-model tests for stepping and all-clear; checked by hand against the named frames
- [x] checks green (`scripts/check.sh`: fmt, clippy -D warnings, tests); commit names ATT-07 and decisions 0016, 0058

### T-0165 · Answer options in the inbox
- **Epic:** M7 · Attention & recovery
